# SP86_C13_149: changes against both witnesses

Material readings and source-state differences only; macro equivalences and editorial page markers are excluded. A resolved C doubt is counted as a reading decision; adding, narrowing or broadening an unresolved doubt alone is excluded. Counts refer to places, so one row may differ from both witnesses. 23 of 23 supplied scans have received the main reader’s review; 23 have also completed the independent image-only reading and crop adjudication.

- Scan 054, lines 3–4, display (2): `1\arrow[r,no head]\&L_i\arrow[r,no head]\arrow[d,phantom,"\simeq"{description,rotate=90}]\&Z(L_i)\arrow[r]\&\Gamma\arrow[r,phantom,"{\unsure{\longrightarrow|-}}"{description}]\&1\\ \&T_\infty(\bar K^*)=T`. The lower T expression is directly beneath L_i, with a vertical relation: the wavy part is on the left and the straight part on the right. The first map is a plain horizontal stroke; the second has only a small terminal downward hook. Z(L_i) to Gamma has a compact visible head. The final Gamma-to-1 tip has a very small hooked spur and remains an open issue. There is no repeated lower L_i and no comma after the final 1. Main-reader decision: preserve the local uncertainty in place, with an uncertain arrow/plain-stroke reading on a phantom horizontal edge; retain the vertical rotated relation.

The crop leaves both its cancellation status and the sequence of insertion/overwriting open; all supported source states are retained.); main line 13, inserted l apostrophe ens/un: `\inserted{l'\unsure{ens|un} des}` (The small ending return can be read as s or as the terminal of n. The leader attachment before L_i is clear.); main line 15, cancelled ns and following dot: `l'e\deleted{\unsure{ns}}\unsure{.|\deleted{.}} fibre` (The l, apostrophe and following e loop stand left of the independent cancellation stroke. That stroke begins over the next low minims and crosses the terminal return, retained as tentative ns. The separate dark dot has uncertain cancellation scope. The next word has f, dotted i, tall b, and a low r/e ending; no separate terminal s or intervening les is visible.); main line 8, shorthand: `$\unsure{=|\overline m}$` (A short detached upper bar is followed by a lower cupped/cusped stroke with a long right return. Its curvature leaves the equals shorthand and compressed barred-letter reading open. The apostrophe in qu'un remains visible. M1155 is reopened; no expansion is introduced.); main line 9, overwritten word: `\deleted{\unsure{de}}` (The underlying d has a pale oval and ascender; the following loop is crossed by the heavy U. The proposed de remains plausible, but the overwritten e is not plain throughout. Local doubt D058_01 is restored.); main line 20, before l'infini: `\unsure{à|:}` (The quotation marks are elevated tick pairs. Between pts and l'infini there is a small lower curved mark and a detached upper stroke. They favor à, while the two-mark form also allows a colon; the uncertainty is retained.)
